    63:         {
    64:             m_Mesh->smMaterialGroup->smMaterial[i].BlendType = SMMAT_BLEND_LAMP;
    65:         }
    66:     }
    67:     return true;
    68: }

   472: int smPATTERN::FindFromCode(DWORD dwCode)
   473: {
   474: 	int cnt;
   475: 
   476: 	for(cnt = 0; cnt < DPAT_MAX; cnt++)

   500: smDPAT *smPATTERN::LoadBipPattern(char *szFileName)
   501: {
   502: 	DWORD dwCode = GetSpeedSum(szFileName);
   503: 	int Num = FindFromCode(dwCode);
   504: 
   505: 	if(Num < 0)
   506: 	{
   507: 
   508: 
   509: 		Num = GetNew();
   510: 		if(Num >= 0)
   511: 		{
   512: 			DinaPat[Num].Pat = smASE_ReadBone(szFileName);
   513: 			if(DinaPat[Num].Pat)
   514: 			{
   515: 				DinaPat[Num].smDinaLink = 0;
   516: 				strcpy_s(DinaPat[Num].szPatName, szFileName);
   517: 				DinaPat[Num].dwSpeedFindSum = dwCode;
   518: 				DinaPat[Num].UseCount = 1;
   519: 				return &DinaPat[Num];
   520: 			}
   521: 		}
   522: 		else
   523: 			return NULL;
   524: 	}
   525: 
   526: 	DinaPat[Num].UseCount++;
   527: 
   528: 	return &DinaPat[Num];
   529: }

   606: 		{
   607: 			for(cnt = 0; cnt < modelInfo.MotionCount; cnt++)
   608: 			{
   609: 				sframe = AnimPat->TmFrame[modelInfo.MotionInfo[cnt].MotionFrame - 1].StartFrame / 160;
   610: 				modelInfo.MotionInfo[cnt].StartFrame += sframe;
   611: 				modelInfo.MotionInfo[cnt].EndFrame += sframe;
   612: 			}
   613: 		}
   614: 
   615: 		DinaPat[Num].lpModelInfo = new smMODELINFO;
   616: 		memcpy(DinaPat[Num].lpModelInfo, &modelInfo, sizeof(smMODELINFO));
   617: 		strcpy_s(DinaPat[Num].szPatName, szFileName);
   618: 		DinaPat[Num].dwSpeedFindSum = dwCode;
   619: 		DinaPat[Num].UseCount = 1;
   620: 		return &DinaPat[Num];
   621: 	}
   622: 
   623: 	DinaPat[Num].UseCount++;
   624: 	return &DinaPat[Num];
   625: }
   626:

   628: extern smPAT3D *PatKnife;
   629: char *szDefaultCharName = "char//woman.ini";
